<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　被执行人未按执行通知履行法律文书确定的义务，应当报告当前以及收到执行通知之日前一年的财产情况。被执行人拒绝报告或者虚假报告的，人民法院可以根据情节轻重对被执行人或者其法定代理人、有关单位的主要负责人或者直接责任人员予以罚款、拘留。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-497" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第497条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-497" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第497条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 252** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations specified in the legal document in accordance with the enforcement notice, the person shall report the current state of property and the state of property for the year prior to the date of receipt of the enforcement notice. If the person subject to execution refuses to report or makes a false report, the People's Court may, depending on the gravity of the circumstances, impose a fine or detention on the person subject to execution, or on the legal representative of the person or the principal leading person or directly responsible person of the relevant unit.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　被执行人未按执行通知履行法律文书确定的义务，人民法院有权向有关单位查询被执行人的存款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产情况。人民法院有权根据不同情形扣押、冻结、划拨、变价被执行人的财产。人民法院查询、扣押、冻结、划拨、变价的财产不得超出被执行人应当履行义务的范围。  
人民法院决定扣押、冻结、划拨、变价财产，应当作出裁定，并发出协助执行通知书，有关单位必须办理。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/文书、送达与废止/最高人民法院关于执行程序中计算迟延履行期间的债务利息适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于执行程序中计算迟延履行期间的债务利息适用法律若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 253** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations specified in the legal document in accordance with the enforcement notice, the People's Court shall have the authority to inquire with relevant units about the property of the person subject to execution, such as deposits, bonds, stocks, fund shares, etc. The People's Court shall have the authority to seize, freeze, transfer, or sell off the property of the person subject to execution under different circumstances. The property inquired about, seized, frozen, transferred, or sold off by the People's Court shall not exceed the scope of the obligations that the person subject to execution shall perform.  
When the People's Court decides to seize, freeze, transfer, or sell off property, it shall issue a ruling and issue a notice of assistance in enforcement, and the relevant unit must comply.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　被执行人未按执行通知履行法律文书确定的义务，人民法院有权扣留、提取被执行人应当履行义务部分的收入。但应当保留被执行人及其所扶养家属的生活必需费用。  
人民法院扣留、提取收入时，应当作出裁定，并发出协助执行通知书，被执行人所在单位、银行、信用合作社和其他有储蓄业务的单位必须办理。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-486" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第486条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-486" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第486条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 254** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations specified in the legal document in accordance with the enforcement notice, the People's Court shall have the authority to withhold or withdraw the portion of the income of the person subject to execution that shall be used to perform the obligations. However, the necessary living expenses for the person subject to execution and the dependents supported by the person shall be reserved.  
When the People's Court withholds or withdraws income, it shall issue a ruling and issue a notice of assistance in enforcement, and the unit where the person subject to execution works, the bank, the credit cooperative, and other units with savings business must comply.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　被执行人未按执行通知履行法律文书确定的义务，人民法院有权查封、扣押、冻结、拍卖、变卖被执行人应当履行义务部分的财产。但应当保留被执行人及其所扶养家属的生活必需品。  
采取前款措施，人民法院应当作出裁定。

**Article 255** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations specified in the legal document in accordance with the enforcement notice, the People's Court shall have the authority to seal up, seize, freeze, auction, or sell off the portion of the property of the person subject to execution that shall be used to perform the obligations. However, the daily necessities for the person subject to execution and the dependents supported by the person shall be reserved.  
When taking the measures provided in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall issue a ruling.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　人民法院查封、扣押财产时，被执行人是公民的，应当通知被执行人或者他的成年家属到场；被执行人是法人或者其他组织的，应当通知其法定代表人或者主要负责人到场。拒不到场的，不影响执行。被执行人是公民的，其工作单位或者财产所在地的基层组织应当派人参加。  
对被查封、扣押的财产，执行员必须造具清单，由在场人签名或者盖章后，交被执行人一份。被执行人是公民的，也可以交他的成年家属一份。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-493" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第493条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-493" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第493条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 256** When the People's Court seals up or seizes property, if the person subject to execution is a citizen, the person subject to execution or his adult family members shall be notified to be present; if the person subject to execution is a legal person or other organization, its legal representative or principal leading person shall be notified to be present. Refusal to be present shall not affect the enforcement. If the person subject to execution is a citizen, the work unit of the person or the grass-roots organization in the place where the property is located shall send personnel to participate.  
For the property sealed up or seized, the enforcement officer must prepare an inventory, which shall be signed or affixed with seals by the persons present, and then a copy shall be given to the person subject to execution. If the person subject to execution is a citizen, a copy may also be given to his adult family members.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　被查封的财产，执行员可以指定被执行人负责保管。因被执行人的过错造成的损失，由被执行人承担。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-28" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第28条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 257** For property sealed up, the enforcement officer may designate the person subject to execution to be responsible for custody. Losses caused by the fault of the person subject to execution shall be borne by the person subject to execution.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　财产被查封、扣押后，执行员应当责令被执行人在指定期间履行法律文书确定的义务。被执行人逾期不履行的，人民法院应当拍卖被查封、扣押的财产；不适于拍卖或者当事人双方同意不进行拍卖的，人民法院可以委托有关单位变卖或者自行变卖。国家禁止自由买卖的物品，交有关单位按照国家规定的价格收购。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-500" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第500条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-500" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第500条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 258** After property is sealed up or seized, the enforcement officer shall order the person subject to execution to perform the obligations specified in the legal document within a specified period. If the person subject to execution fails to perform within the extended period, the People's Court shall auction the sealed-up or seized property; if the property is not suitable for auction or if the parties agree not to conduct an auction, the People's Court may entrust a relevant unit to sell off the property or sell it off itself. Articles prohibited by the State from free trading shall be delivered to the relevant unit for purchase at the price prescribed by the State.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　被执行人不履行法律文书确定的义务，并隐匿财产的，人民法院有权发出搜查令，对被执行人及其住所或者财产隐匿地进行搜查。  
采取前款措施，由院长签发搜查令。

**Article 259** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations specified in the legal document and conceals property, the People's Court shall have the authority to issue a search warrant to search the person subject to execution and the premises of the person or the place where property is concealed.  
When taking the measures provided in the preceding paragraph, the search warrant shall be signed by the president of the court.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　法律文书指定交付的财物或者票证，由执行员传唤双方当事人当面交付，或者由执行员转交，并由被交付人签收。  
有关单位持有该项财物或者票证的，应当根据人民法院的协助执行通知书转交，并由被交付人签收。  
有关公民持有该项财物或者票证的，人民法院通知其交出。拒不交出的，强制执行。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-480" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第480条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/律师、仲裁与公证/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国仲裁法》若干问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国仲裁法》若干问题的解释》第17条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-480" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第480条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 260** For money or goods or negotiable instruments designated for delivery in a legal document, the enforcement officer shall summon the parties to deliver them face to face, or the enforcement officer shall transfer them, and the person to whom they are delivered shall sign a receipt.  
If a relevant unit holds such money or goods or negotiable instruments, it shall transfer them in accordance with the notice of assistance in enforcement of the People's Court, and the person to whom they are delivered shall sign a receipt.  
If a relevant citizen holds such money or goods or negotiable instruments, the People's Court shall notify the citizen to surrender them. If the citizen refuses to surrender them, compulsory execution shall be carried out.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　强制迁出房屋或者强制退出土地，由院长签发公告，责令被执行人在指定期间履行。被执行人逾期不履行的，由执行员强制执行。  
强制执行时，被执行人是公民的，应当通知被执行人或者他的成年家属到场；被执行人是法人或者其他组织的，应当通知其法定代表人或者主要负责人到场。拒不到场的，不影响执行。被执行人是公民的，其工作单位或者房屋、土地所在地的基层组织应当派人参加。执行员应当将强制执行情况记入笔录，由在场人签名或者盖章。  
强制迁出房屋被搬出的财物，由人民法院派人运至指定处所，交给被执行人。被执行人是公民的，也可以交给他的成年家属。因拒绝接收而造成的损失，由被执行人承担。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-515" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第515条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-515" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第515条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 261** For compulsory removal from a house or compulsory vacation of land, the president of the court shall issue a public notice, ordering the person subject to execution to perform within a specified period. If the person subject to execution fails to perform within the extended period, the enforcement officer shall carry out compulsory execution.  
During compulsory execution, if the person subject to execution is a citizen, the person subject to execution or his adult family members shall be notified to be present; if the person subject to execution is a legal person or other organization, its legal representative or principal leading person shall be notified to be present. Refusal to be present shall not affect the enforcement. If the person subject to execution is a citizen, the work unit of the person or the grass-roots organization in the place where the house or land is located shall send personnel to participate. The enforcement officer shall record the circumstances of the compulsory execution in the笔录, which shall be signed or affixed with seals by the persons present.  
The property removed during compulsory removal from the house shall be transported by personnel of the People's Court to a designated location and delivered to the person subject to execution. If the person subject to execution is a citizen, it may also be delivered to his adult family members. Losses caused by refusal to accept delivery shall be borne by the person subject to execution.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　在执行中，需要办理有关财产权证照转移手续的，人民法院可以向有关单位发出协助执行通知书，有关单位必须办理。

**Article 262** During enforcement, if it is necessary to go through the formalities for the transfer of certificates of property rights, the People's Court may issue a notice of assistance in enforcement to the relevant unit, and the relevant unit must comply.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　对判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的行为，被执行人未按执行通知履行的，人民法院可以强制执行或者委托有关单位或者其他人完成，费用由被执行人承担。

**Article 263** For acts designated in judgments, rulings, or other legal documents, if the person subject to execution fails to perform in accordance with the enforcement notice, the People's Court may carry out compulsory execution or entrust the act to a relevant unit or other person to complete, and the expenses shall be borne by the person subject to execution.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　被执行人未按判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的期间履行给付金钱义务的，应当加倍支付迟延履行期间的债务利息。被执行人未按判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的期间履行其他义务的，应当支付迟延履行金。

**Article 264** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligation to pay money within the period specified in a judgment, ruling, or other legal document, the person shall pay double the interest on the debt for the period of delayed performance. If the person subject to execution fails to perform other obligations within the period specified in a judgment, ruling, or other legal document, the person shall pay a surcharge for delayed performance.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　人民法院采取[本法第二百五十三条](#art-253)、第二百五十四条、第二百五十五条规定的执行措施后，被执行人仍不能偿还债务的，应当继续履行义务。债权人发现被执行人有其他财产的，可以随时请求人民法院执行。

**Article 265** After the People's Court has taken the enforcement measures provided for in Article 253, Article 254, and Article 255 of this Law, if the person subject to execution is still unable to repay the debt, the person shall continue to perform the obligation. If the creditor discovers that the person subject to execution has other property, the creditor may at any time request the People's Court to enforce.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　被执行人不履行法律文书确定的义务的，人民法院可以对其采取或者通知有关单位协助采取限制出境，在征信系统记录、通过媒体公布不履行义务信息以及法律规定的其他措施。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/海事与运输/最高人民法院关于审理船舶碰撞纠纷案件若干问题的规定.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理船舶碰撞纠纷案件若干问题的规定》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 266** If the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations specified in the legal document, the People's Court may impose or notify the relevant unit to assist in imposing measures such as restricting the person from leaving the country, recording the information in the credit reference system, publishing the information of non-performance through media, and other measures provided by law.

### 第二十二章　执行中止和终结

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定中止执行：  
（一）申请人表示可以延期执行的；  
（二）案外人对执行标的提出确有理由的异议的；  
（三）作为一方当事人的公民死亡，需要等待继承人继承权利或者承担义务的；  
（四）作为一方当事人的法人或者其他组织终止，尚未确定权利义务承受人的；  
（五）人民法院认为应当中止执行的其他情形。  
中止的情形消失后，恢复执行。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/文书、送达与废止/最高人民法院关于当事人申请承认澳大利亚法院出具的离婚证明书人民法院应否受理问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于当事人申请承认澳大利亚法院出具的离婚证明书人民法院应否受理问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 267** In any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall rule to suspend enforcement:  
(1) The applicant indicates that enforcement may be postponed;  
(2) A person not involved in the case raises a well-founded objection to the subject matter of enforcement;  
(3) A citizen who is a party dies and it is necessary to await the successor to succeed to the rights or assume the obligations;  
(4) A legal person or other organization that is a party terminates and the successor of rights and obligations has not yet been determined;  
(5) Other circumstances where the People's Court considers that enforcement shall be suspended.  
Enforcement shall resume after the circumstances for suspension disappear.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　有下列情形之一的，人民法院裁定终结执行：  
（一）申请人撤销申请的；  
（二）据以执行的法律文书被撤销的；  
（三）作为被执行人的公民死亡，无遗产可供执行，又无义务承担人的；  
（四）追索赡养费、扶养费、抚养费案件的权利人死亡的；  
（五）作为被执行人的公民因生活困难无力偿还借款，无收入来源，又丧失劳动能力的；  
（六）人民法院认为应当终结执行的其他情形。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国海事诉讼特别程序法》若干问题的解释.md#art-88" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国海事诉讼特别程序法》若干问题的解释》第88条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 268** In any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall rule to terminate enforcement:  
(1) The applicant withdraws the application;  
(2) The legal document on the basis of which enforcement is carried out is revoked;  
(3) The citizen who is the person subject to execution dies, leaving no estate for enforcement and no person to assume the obligation;  
(4) The right holder in a case for recovery of alimony, support, or child support dies;  
(5) The citizen who is the person subject to execution is unable to repay the loan due to financial difficulties, has no source of income, and has lost the ability to work;  
(6) Other circumstances where the People's Court considers that enforcement shall be terminated.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　中止和终结执行的裁定，送达当事人后立即生效。

**Article 269** The ruling to suspend or terminate enforcement shall take immediate effect upon service on the parties.

## 第四编　涉外民事诉讼程序的特别规定

### 第二十三章　一般原则

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　在中华人民共和国领域内进行涉外民事诉讼，适用本编规定。本编没有规定的，适用本法其他有关规定。

**Article 270** Civil actions involving foreign element conducted within the territory of the People's Republic of China shall be governed by the provisions of this Part. Where this Part does not provide otherwise, the other relevant provisions of this Law shall apply.

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约同本法有不同规定的，适用该国际条约的规定，但中华人民共和国声明保留的条款除外。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-522" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第522条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-522" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第522条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 271** Where an international treaty concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China contains provisions that differ from those of this Law, the provisions of that international treaty shall apply, except for the provisions on which the People's Republic of China has declared reservations.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　对享有外交特权与豁免的外国人、外国组织或者国际组织提起的民事诉讼，应当依照中华人民共和国有关法律和中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约的规定办理。

**Article 272** Civil actions instituted against a foreign national, foreign organization, or international organization enjoying diplomatic privileges and immunities shall be handled in accordance with the relevant laws of the People's Republic of China and the provisions of international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　人民法院审理涉外民事案件，应当使用中华人民共和国通用的语言、文字。当事人要求提供翻译的，可以提供，费用由当事人承担。

**Article 273** When adjudicating a civil case involving foreign element, the People's Court shall use the spoken and written languages commonly used in the People's Republic of China. If a party requests the provision of interpretation, it may be provided, and the expenses shall be borne by the party.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　外国人、无国籍人、外国企业和组织在人民法院起诉、应诉，需要委托律师代理诉讼的，必须委托中华人民共和国的律师。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-532" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第532条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/文书、送达与废止/最高人民法院关于审理仲裁司法审查案件若干问题的规定.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理仲裁司法审查案件若干问题的规定》第17条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-532" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第532条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 274** If a foreign national, stateless person, foreign enterprise, or organization instructs a lawyer to represent the party in prosecution or defense before the People's Court, the party must instruct a lawyer of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的外国人、无国籍人、外国企业和组织委托中华人民共和国律师或者其他人代理诉讼，从中华人民共和国领域外寄交或者托交的授权委托书，应当经所在国公证机关证明，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续后，才具有效力。

**Article 275** If a foreign national, stateless person, foreign enterprise, or organization with no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China instructs a lawyer of the People's Republic of China or any other person to represent the party in litigation, the power of attorney sent or entrusted from outside the territory of the People's Republic of China shall become effective only after it is notarized by a notarial authority in the country of domicile and authenticated by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or after the certification formalities prescribed in the relevant treaty concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country are fulfilled.

### 第二十四章　管辖

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　因涉外民事纠纷，对在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的被告提起除身份关系以外的诉讼，如果合同签订地、合同履行地、诉讼标的物所在地、可供扣押财产所在地、侵权行为地、代表机构住所地位于中华人民共和国领域内的，可以由合同签订地、合同履行地、诉讼标的物所在地、可供扣押财产所在地、侵权行为地、代表机构住所地人民法院管辖。  
除前款规定外，涉外民事纠纷与中华人民共和国存在其他适当联系的，可以由人民法院管辖。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-536" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第536条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-536" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第536条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理垄断民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理垄断民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 276** For a civil dispute involving foreign element, if an action is instituted against a defendant with no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, other than an action concerning personal status, and if the place of contracting of the contract, the place of performance of the contract, the location of the subject matter of the action, the location of the property available for seizure, the location of the tortious act, or the location of the representative office is within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the action may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place of contracting, place of performance, location of the subject matter, location of property available for seizure, location of the tortious act, or location of the representative office.  
In addition to the provisions of the preceding paragraph, if a civil dispute involving foreign element has any other appropriate connection with the People's Republic of China, it may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　涉外民事纠纷的当事人书面协议选择人民法院管辖的，可以由人民法院管辖。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/文书、送达与废止/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于设立国际商事法庭若干问题的规定》的决定.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于设立国际商事法庭若干问题的规定》的决定》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 277** If the parties to a civil dispute involving foreign element agree in writing to choose the People's Court for jurisdiction, the dispute may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　当事人未提出管辖异议，并应诉答辩或者提出反诉的，视为人民法院有管辖权。

**Article 278** If a party does not raise an objection to jurisdiction and responds to the action or files a counterclaim, the People's Court shall be deemed to have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　下列民事案件，由人民法院专属管辖：  
（一）因在中华人民共和国领域内设立的法人或者其他组织的设立、解散、清算，以及该法人或者其他组织作出的决议的效力等纠纷提起的诉讼；  
（二）因与在中华人民共和国领域内审查授予的知识产权的有效性有关的纠纷提起的诉讼；  
（三）因在中华人民共和国领域内履行中外合资经营企业合同、中外合作经营企业合同、中外合作勘探开发自然资源合同发生纠纷提起的诉讼。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-540" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第540条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-540" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第540条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 279** The following civil cases shall be under the exclusive jurisdiction of the People's Court:  
(1) Actions instituted arising from disputes over the establishment, dissolution, or liquidation of a legal person or other organization established within the territory of the People's Republic of China, or over the validity of resolutions made by such legal person or other organization;  
(2) Actions instituted arising from disputes relating to the validity of intellectual property rights examined and granted within the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(3) Actions instituted arising from disputes over the performance of contracts for Chinese-foreign equity joint ventures, contracts for Chinese-foreign contractual joint ventures, or contracts for Chinese-foreign cooperative exploration and development of natural resources within the territory of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　当事人之间的同一纠纷，一方当事人向外国法院起诉，另一方当事人向人民法院起诉，或者一方当事人既向外国法院起诉，又向人民法院起诉，人民法院依照本法有管辖权的，可以受理。当事人订立排他性管辖协议选择外国法院管辖且不违反本法对专属管辖的规定，不涉及中华人民共和国主权、安全或者社会公共利益的，人民法院可以裁定不予受理；已经受理的，裁定驳回起诉。

**Article 280** For the same dispute between the parties, if one party institutes an action in a foreign court and the other party institutes an action in the People's Court, or if one party institutes an action both in a foreign court and in the People's Court, the People's Court may accept the case if it has jurisdiction according to this Law. If the parties have entered into an exclusive jurisdiction agreement choosing the jurisdiction of a foreign court and this does not violate the provisions of this Law on exclusive jurisdiction and does not involve the sovereignty, security, or public interest of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may rule not to accept the case; if it has already accepted the case, it shall rule to dismiss the action.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　人民法院依据前条规定受理案件后，当事人以外国法院已经先于人民法院受理为由，书面申请人民法院中止诉讼的，人民法院可以裁定中止诉讼，但是存在下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）当事人协议选择人民法院管辖，或者纠纷属于人民法院专属管辖；  
（二）由人民法院审理明显更为方便。  
外国法院未采取必要措施审理案件，或者未在合理期限内审结的，依当事人的书面申请，人民法院应当恢复诉讼。  
外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，已经被人民法院全部或者部分承认，当事人对已经获得承认的部分又向人民法院起诉的，裁定不予受理；已经受理的，裁定驳回起诉。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-539" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第539条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-539" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第539条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 281** If, after the People's Court accepts a case pursuant to the preceding article, a party applies in writing for the People's Court to suspend the action on the ground that a foreign court has already accepted the case prior to the People's Court, the People's Court may order suspension of the action, unless any of the following circumstances exists:  
(1) the parties have agreed by agreement to choose the jurisdiction of the People's Court, or the dispute falls within the exclusive jurisdiction of the People's Court;  
(2) it is manifestly more convenient for the People's Court to adjudicate the case.  
If the foreign court fails to take necessary measures to adjudicate the case or fails to conclude the case within a reasonable period, upon the party's written application, the People's Court shall resume the action.  
If a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a foreign court has been wholly or partially recognized by the People's Court, and the party institutes a new action before the People's Court with respect to the part that has been recognized, the People's Court shall rule that the prosecution is not to be accepted; if it has already been accepted, the People's Court shall rule that the prosecution is to be dismissed.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　人民法院受理的涉外民事案件，被告提出管辖异议，且同时有下列情形的，可以裁定驳回起诉，告知原告向更为方便的外国法院提起诉讼：  
（一）案件争议的基本事实不是发生在中华人民共和国领域内，人民法院审理案件和当事人参加诉讼均明显不方便；  
（二）当事人之间不存在选择人民法院管辖的协议；  
（三）案件不属于人民法院专属管辖；  
（四）案件不涉及中华人民共和国主权、安全或者社会公共利益；  
（五）外国法院审理案件更为方便。  
裁定驳回起诉后，外国法院对纠纷拒绝行使管辖权，或者未采取必要措施审理案件，或者未在合理期限内审结，当事人又向人民法院起诉的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 282** If, in a foreign-related civil action accepted by the People's Court, the defendant raises an objection to jurisdiction and the following circumstances concurrently exist, the People's Court may rule to dismiss the prosecution and inform the plaintiff to institute an action before a more convenient foreign court:  
(1) the basic facts in dispute of the case do not occur within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and it is manifestly inconvenient for the People's Court to adjudicate the case and for the parties to participate in the action;  
(2) there is no agreement between the parties choosing the jurisdiction of the People's Court;  
(3) the case does not fall within the exclusive jurisdiction of the People's Court;  
(4) the case does not involve the sovereignty, security, or public interests of the People's Republic of China;  
(5) it is more convenient for a foreign court to adjudicate the case.  
After the prosecution is dismissed by ruling, if the foreign court refuses to exercise jurisdiction over the dispute, or fails to take necessary measures to adjudicate the case, or fails to conclude the case within a reasonable period, and the party institutes an action before the People's Court again, the People's Court shall accept the case.

### 第二十五章　送达、调查取证、期间

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　人民法院对在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的当事人送达诉讼文书，可以采用下列方式：  
（一）依照受送达人所在国与中华人民共和国缔结或者共同参加的国际条约中规定的方式送达；  
（二）通过外交途径送达；  
（三）对具有中华人民共和国国籍的受送达人，可以委托中华人民共和国驻受送达人所在国的使领馆代为送达；  
（四）向受送达人在本案中委托的诉讼代理人送达；  
（五）向受送达人在中华人民共和国领域内设立的独资企业、代表机构、分支机构或者有权接受送达的业务代办人送达；  
（六）受送达人为外国人、无国籍人，其在中华人民共和国领域内设立的法人或者其他组织担任法定代表人或者主要负责人，且与该法人或者其他组织为共同被告的，向该法人或者其他组织送达；  
（七）受送达人为外国法人或者其他组织，其法定代表人或者主要负责人在中华人民共和国领域内的，向其法定代表人或者主要负责人送达；  
（八）受送达人所在国的法律允许邮寄送达的，可以邮寄送达，自邮寄之日起满三个月，送达回证没有退回，但根据各种情况足以认定已经送达的，期间届满之日视为送达；  
（九）采用能够确认受送达人收悉的电子方式送达，但是受送达人所在国法律禁止的除外；  
（十）以受送达人同意的其他方式送达，但是受送达人所在国法律禁止的除外。  
不能用上述方式送达的，公告送达，自发出公告之日起，经过六十日，即视为送达。

**Article 283** The People's Court may use the following methods for service of litigation documents on a party who has no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China:  
(1) service in the manner prescribed in the international treaties concluded or jointly acceded to by the country where the person on whom service is to be made is located and the People's Republic of China;  
(2) service through diplomatic channels;  
(3) for a person on whom service is to be made who is a national of the People's Republic of China, entrusting the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in the country where the person is located to serve on the person's behalf;  
(4) service on the litigation agent authorized by the person on whom service is to be made in the case;  
(5) service on the wholly-owned enterprise, representative office, branch office, or business agent authorized to accept service established by the person on whom service is to be made within the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(6) if the person on whom service is to be made is a foreigner or a stateless person, who serves as the legal representative or principal responsible person of a legal person or other organization established within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and is a co-defendant with such legal person or other organization, service on such legal person or other organization;  
(7) if the person on whom service is to be made is a foreign legal person or other organization, whose legal representative or principal responsible person is within the territory of the People's Republic of China, service on such legal representative or principal responsible person;  
(8) if the law of the country where the person on whom service is to be made is located permits service by mail, service may be made by mail; if the receipt of service is not returned after three full months from the date of mailing, but it can be sufficiently determined based on all circumstances that service has been made, the date upon which the period expires shall be deemed the date of service;  
(9) service by electronic means that can confirm receipt by the person on whom service is to be made, unless prohibited by the law of the country where the person is located;  
(10) service by other means agreed by the person on whom service is to be made, unless prohibited by the law of the country where the person is located.  
If none of the above methods of service can be used, service by public announcement shall be made; sixty days after the date of the issuance of the announcement, service shall be deemed to have been made.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　当事人申请人民法院调查收集的证据位于中华人民共和国领域外，人民法院可以依照证据所在国与中华人民共和国缔结或者共同参加的国际条约中规定的方式，或者通过外交途径调查收集。  
在所在国法律不禁止的情况下，人民法院可以采用下列方式调查收集：  
（一）对具有中华人民共和国国籍的当事人、证人，可以委托中华人民共和国驻当事人、证人所在国的使领馆代为取证；  
（二）经双方当事人同意，通过即时通讯工具取证；  
（三）以双方当事人同意的其他方式取证。

**Article 284** If the evidence that a party applies to the People's Court to investigate and collect is located outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may investigate and collect it in the manner prescribed in the international treaties concluded or jointly acceded to by the country where the evidence is located and the People's Republic of China, or through diplomatic channels.  
If not prohibited by the law of the country where the evidence is located, the People's Court may investigate and collect evidence using the following methods:  
(1) for a party or witness who is a national of the People's Republic of China, entrusting the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in the country where such party or witness is located to take evidence on its behalf;  
(2) with the consent of both parties, taking evidence through instant communication tools;  
(3) taking evidence by other means agreed by both parties.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　被告在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的，人民法院应当将起诉状副本送达被告，并通知被告在收到起诉状副本后三十日内提出答辩状。被告申请延期的，是否准许，由人民法院决定。

**Article 285** If the defendant has no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall serve a copy of the statement of complaint on the defendant and notify the defendant to submit a statement of defense within thirty days after receipt of the copy of the statement of complaint. Whether an extension applied for by the defendant shall be granted shall be decided by the People's Court.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的当事人，不服第一审人民法院判决、裁定的，有权在判决书、裁定书送达之日起三十日内提起上诉。被上诉人在收到上诉状副本后，应当在三十日内提出答辩状。当事人不能在法定期间提起上诉或者提出答辩状，申请延期的，是否准许，由人民法院决定。

**Article 286** A party who has no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China and who is dissatisfied with a judgment or ruling of a first-instance People's Court shall have the right to file an appeal within thirty days from the date of service of the judgment or ruling. The respondent shall, after receiving a copy of the statement of appeal, submit a statement of defense within thirty days. Whether an extension applied for by a party who is unable to file an appeal or submit a statement of defense within the statutory period shall be granted shall be decided by the People's Court.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　人民法院审理涉外民事案件的期间，不受[本法第一百五十二条](#art-152)、第一百八十三条规定的限制。

**Article 287** The time periods for the People's Court to adjudicate foreign-related civil actions shall not be subject to the restrictions provided for in Articles 152 and 183 of this Law.

### 第二十六章　仲裁

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　涉外经济贸易、运输和海事中发生的纠纷，当事人在合同中订有仲裁条款或者事后达成书面仲裁协议，提交中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构或者其他仲裁机构仲裁的，当事人不得向人民法院起诉。  
当事人在合同中没有订有仲裁条款或者事后没有达成书面仲裁协议的，可以向人民法院起诉。

**Article 288** For disputes arising from foreign-related economic and trade, transport, and maritime activities, if the parties have included an arbitration clause in the contract or subsequently concluded a written arbitration agreement to submit the dispute for arbitration to a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China or another arbitration institution, the parties shall not institute an action before the People's Court.  
If the parties have not included an arbitration clause in the contract or have not subsequently concluded a written arbitration agreement, they may institute an action before the People's Court.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　当事人申请采取保全的，中华人民共和国的涉外仲裁机构应当将当事人的申请，提交被申请人住所地或者财产所在地的中级人民法院裁定。

**Article 289** If a party applies for preservation measures, the foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China shall submit the party's application to the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the respondent has its domicile or where the property is located for a ruling.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　经中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构裁决的，当事人不得向人民法院起诉。一方当事人不履行仲裁裁决的，对方当事人可以向被申请人住所地或者财产所在地的中级人民法院申请执行。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-543" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第543条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定.md#art-543" title="被《最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定》第543条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 290** If a dispute has been arbitrated by a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China, the parties shall not institute an action before the People's Court. If one party fails to perform the arbitration award, the other party may apply for enforcement to the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the respondent has its domicile or where the property is located.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　对中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构作出的裁决，被申请人提出证据证明仲裁裁决有下列情形之一的，经人民法院组成合议庭审查核实，裁定不予执行：  
（一）当事人在合同中没有订有仲裁条款或者事后没有达成书面仲裁协议的；  
（二）被申请人没有得到指定仲裁员或者进行仲裁程序的通知，或者由于其他不属于被申请人负责的原因未能陈述意见的；  
（三）仲裁庭的组成或者仲裁的程序与仲裁规则不符的；  
（四）裁决的事项不属于仲裁协议的范围或者仲裁机构无权仲裁的。  
人民法院认定执行该裁决违背社会公共利益的，裁定不予执行。

**Article 291** For an award made by a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China, if the respondent presents evidence proving that the arbitration award falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall, after examination and verification by a collegial panel formed by the People's Court, rule that the award shall not be enforced:  
(1) the parties have not included an arbitration clause in the contract or have not subsequently concluded a written arbitration agreement;  
(2) the respondent has not received notice of the appointment of an arbitrator or of the arbitration proceedings, or was unable to present its case due to reasons for which the respondent is not responsible;  
(3) the composition of the arbitration tribunal or the arbitration procedure was not in accordance with the arbitration rules;  
(4) the matters decided in the award fall outside the scope of the arbitration agreement or the arbitration institution has no authority to arbitrate.  
If the People's Court determines that the enforcement of the award would be contrary to the social and public interests, the People's Court shall rule that the award shall not be enforced.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　仲裁裁决被人民法院裁定不予执行的，当事人可以根据双方达成的书面仲裁协议重新申请仲裁，也可以向人民法院起诉。

**Article 292** If an arbitration award is ruled by the People's Court not to be enforced, the parties may apply for arbitration again pursuant to a written arbitration agreement reached between them, or may institute an action before the People's Court.

### 第二十七章　司法协助

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　根据中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约，或者按照互惠原则，人民法院和外国法院可以相互请求，代为送达文书、调查取证以及进行其他诉讼行为。  
外国法院请求协助的事项有损于中华人民共和国的主权、安全或者社会公共利益的，人民法院不予执行。

**Article 293** Pursuant to international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, or in accordance with the principle of reciprocity, the People's Court and a foreign court may request each other to serve documents, investigate and collect evidence, and carry out other litigation acts on their behalf.  
If the matters for which a foreign court requests assistance are detrimental to the sovereignty, security, or social and public interests of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall not execute them.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　请求和提供司法协助，应当依照中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约所规定的途径进行；没有条约关系的，通过外交途径进行。  
外国驻中华人民共和国的使领馆可以向该国公民送达文书和调查取证，但不得违反中华人民共和国的法律，并不得采取强制措施。  
除前款规定的情况外，未经中华人民共和国主管机关准许，任何外国机关或者个人不得在中华人民共和国领域内送达文书、调查取证。

**Article 294** Requests for and provision of judicial assistance shall be carried out through the channels provided for in international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China; in the absence of such treaty relations, they shall be carried out through diplomatic channels.  
An embassy or consulate of a foreign country in the People's Republic of China may serve documents and investigate and collect evidence on its nationals, provided that the laws of the People's Republic of China are not violated and no compulsory measures are taken.  
Except as provided for in the preceding paragraph, no foreign organ or individual shall serve documents or investigate and collect evidence within the territory of the People's Republic of China without the permission of the competent authorities of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　外国法院请求人民法院提供司法协助的请求书及其所附文件，应当附有中文译本或者国际条约规定的其他文字文本。  
人民法院请求外国法院提供司法协助的请求书及其所附文件，应当附有该国文字译本或者国际条约规定的其他文字文本。

**Article 295** A request letter submitted by a foreign court to the People's Court for judicial assistance and the documents attached thereto shall be accompanied by a Chinese translation or a version in another language as prescribed by an international treaty.  
A request letter submitted by the People's Court to a foreign court for judicial assistance and the documents attached thereto shall be accompanied by a translation in the language of that country or a version in another language as prescribed by an international treaty.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　人民法院提供司法协助，依照中华人民共和国法律规定的程序进行。外国法院请求采用特殊方式的，也可以按照其请求的特殊方式进行，但请求采用的特殊方式不得违反中华人民共和国法律。

**Article 296** When providing judicial assistance, the People's Court shall proceed in accordance with the procedures prescribed by the laws of the People's Republic of China. If a foreign court requests that a special method be used, it may also be carried out in the special method requested, provided that the special method requested does not violate the laws of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　人民法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，如果被执行人或者其财产不在中华人民共和国领域内，当事人请求执行的，可以由当事人直接向有管辖权的外国法院申请承认和执行，也可以由人民法院依照中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约的规定，或者按照互惠原则，请求外国法院承认和执行。  
在中华人民共和国领域内依法作出的发生法律效力的仲裁裁决，当事人请求执行的，如果被执行人或者其财产不在中华人民共和国领域内，当事人可以直接向有管辖权的外国法院申请承认和执行。

**Article 297** If, with respect to a legally effective judgment or ruling made by the People's Court, the person subject to enforcement or its property is not within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and a party requests enforcement, the party may directly apply to a competent foreign court for recognition and enforcement, or the People's Court may request the foreign court for recognition and enforcement in accordance with the provisions of international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, or in accordance with the principle of reciprocity.  
If a legally effective arbitration award is made in accordance with law within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and a party requests enforcement, and the person subject to enforcement or its property is not within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the party may directly apply to a competent foreign court for recognition and enforcement.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，需要人民法院承认和执行的，可以由当事人直接向有管辖权的中级人民法院申请承认和执行，也可以由外国法院依照该国与中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约的规定，或者按照互惠原则，请求人民法院承认和执行。

**Article 298** If a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a foreign court requires recognition and enforcement by the People's Court, a party may directly apply to a competent Intermediate People's Court for recognition and enforcement, or the foreign court may request the People's Court for recognition and enforcement in accordance with the provisions of international treaties concluded or acceded to by that country and the People's Republic of China, or in accordance with the principle of reciprocity.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　人民法院对申请或者请求承认和执行的外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，依照中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约，或者按照互惠原则进行审查后，认为不违反中华人民共和国法律的基本原则且不损害国家主权、安全、社会公共利益的，裁定承认其效力；需要执行的，发出执行令，依照本法的有关规定执行。

**Article 299** After examining a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a foreign court for which recognition and enforcement is applied for or requested, the People's Court, in accordance with international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, or in accordance with the principle of reciprocity, shall rule to recognize its validity if it considers that the judgment or ruling does not violate the fundamental principles of the laws of the People's Republic of China and does not harm national sovereignty, security, or social and public interests; if enforcement is required, the People's Court shall issue an enforcement order and enforce it in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Law.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　对申请或者请求承认和执行的外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，人民法院经审查，有下列情形之一的，裁定不予承认和执行：  
（一）依据[本法第三百零一条](#art-301)的规定，外国法院对案件无管辖权；  
（二）被申请人未得到合法传唤或者虽经合法传唤但未获得合理的陈述、辩论机会，或者无诉讼行为能力的当事人未得到适当代理；  
（三）判决、裁定是通过欺诈方式取得；  
（四）人民法院已对同一纠纷作出判决、裁定，或者已经承认第三国法院对同一纠纷作出的判决、裁定；  
（五）违反中华人民共和国法律的基本原则或者损害国家主权、安全、社会公共利益。

**Article 300** With respect to a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a foreign court for which recognition and enforcement is applied for or requested, the People's Court shall, after examination, rule not to recognize and enforce it if any of the following circumstances exists:  
(1) in accordance with the provisions of Article 301 of this Law, the foreign court has no jurisdiction over the case;  
(2) the respondent has not been legally summoned, or although legally summoned, has not been given a reasonable opportunity to present its case or to be heard, or a party who lacks legal capacity has not been properly represented;  
(3) the judgment or ruling was obtained by fraud;  
(4) the People's Court has already made a judgment or ruling on the same dispute, or has already recognized a judgment or ruling made by a court of a third state on the same dispute;  
(5) the judgment or ruling violates the fundamental principles of the laws of the People's Republic of China or harms national sovereignty, security, or social and public interests.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当认定该外国法院对案件无管辖权：  
（一）外国法院依照其法律对案件没有管辖权，或者虽然依照其法律有管辖权但与案件所涉纠纷无适当联系；  
（二）违反本法对专属管辖的规定；  
（三）违反当事人排他性选择法院管辖的协议。

**Article 301** The People's Court shall determine that a foreign court has no jurisdiction over a case if any of the following circumstances exists:  
(1) the foreign court has no jurisdiction over the case under its own law, or although it has jurisdiction under its own law, it has no appropriate connection with the dispute involved in the case;  
(2) the foreign court's jurisdiction violates the provisions of this Law on exclusive jurisdiction;  
(3) the foreign court's jurisdiction violates an agreement between the parties exclusively selecting the jurisdiction of a court.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　当事人向人民法院申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，该判决、裁定涉及的纠纷与人民法院正在审理的纠纷属于同一纠纷的，人民法院可以裁定中止诉讼。  
外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定不符合本法规定的承认条件的，人民法院裁定不予承认和执行，并恢复已经中止的诉讼；符合本法规定的承认条件的，人民法院裁定承认其效力；需要执行的，发出执行令，依照本法的有关规定执行；对已经中止的诉讼，裁定驳回起诉。

**Article 302** If a party applies to the People's Court for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a foreign court, and the dispute involved in such judgment or ruling is the same as the dispute being adjudicated by the People's Court, the People's Court may order suspension of the action.  
If a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a foreign court does not meet the conditions for recognition provided for in this Law, the People's Court shall rule not to recognize and enforce it, and shall resume the action that has been suspended; if it meets the conditions for recognition provided for in this Law, the People's Court shall rule to recognize its validity; if enforcement is required, the People's Court shall issue an enforcement order and enforce it in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Law; with respect to the action that has been suspended, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the prosecution.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　当事人对承认和执行或者不予承认和执行的裁定不服的，可以自裁定送达之日起十日内向上一级人民法院申请复议。

**Article 303** If a party is dissatisfied with a ruling on recognition and enforcement or on non-recognition and non-enforcement, it may apply for reconsideration to the People's Court at the next higher level within ten days from the date of service of the ruling.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　在中华人民共和国领域外作出的发生法律效力的仲裁裁决，需要人民法院承认和执行的，当事人可以直接向被执行人住所地或者其财产所在地的中级人民法院申请。被执行人住所地或者其财产不在中华人民共和国领域内的，当事人可以向申请人住所地或者与裁决的纠纷有适当联系的地点的中级人民法院申请。人民法院应当依照中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约，或者按照互惠原则办理。

**Article 304** If a legally effective arbitration award made outside the territory of the People's Republic of China requires recognition and enforcement by the People's Court, a party may directly apply to the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the person subject to enforcement has its domicile or where its property is located. If the domicile of the person subject to enforcement or its property is not within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the party may apply to the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the applicant has its domicile or of the place that has an appropriate connection with the dispute in the award. The People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, or in accordance with the principle of reciprocity.

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　涉及外国国家的民事诉讼，适用中华人民共和国有关外国国家豁免的法律规定；有关法律没有规定的，适用本法。

**Article 305** Civil actions involving foreign states shall be governed by the legal provisions of the People's Republic of China on foreign state immunity; where there are no such provisions in the relevant laws, this Law shall apply.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　本法自公布之日起施行，《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法（试行）》同时废止。

**Article 306** This Law shall come into force as of the date of its promulgation, and the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China (for Trial Implementation) shall be abolished simultaneously.
